Here, to obtain
(
a
)
, we used Poincar
\displaystyle\acute{\text{e}}
inequality, and to
(
b
)
, we used
Corollary

5.8
applied with
\displaystyle s=2\rho
and
\displaystyle r=3\rho
. Finally, an application of Young’s inequality gives the desired estimate.
∎

Lemma 5.11
.

Assuming
Lemma

5.4
holds and with notation as in
Lemma

5.7
, for any
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, the following holds:

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)|\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}.

Recall that
\displaystyle q
is from
Definition

5.2
.

Proof.

From Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)|\ dz\\
&&\leq\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}(2\rho)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\fint_{I_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}}\ dt\right)\\
&&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{le_la}}{\leq}\lambda^{p-1}\left(\fint_{I_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}}\ dt\right).\end{array}

(5.7)

In order to estimate the integral term appearing on the right-hand side of
Eq.

5.7
, we consider two subcases and proceed as follows.

Case
\displaystyle p\leq n
:

In this case, we can directly apply
Lemma

4.5
to
Eq.

5.7
to get

\left(\fint_{I_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}}\ dt\right)\apprle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}.

Case
\displaystyle p>n
:

We will further restrict
\displaystyle\delta
such that
\displaystyle\delta>\frac{n}{p}
which implies
\displaystyle q>n
. Similarly, calculation from
Lemma

5.6
gives

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)|\ dz&\leq&\fint_{I_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}(2\rho)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\sup_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|\right)\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{5.27a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\apprle}&\fint_{I_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}(2\rho)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{5.27b}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\apprle}&\lambda^{p-1}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}},\end{array}

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we proceeded analogous to
Eq.

5.6
noting that
\displaystyle q>n
by the restriction on
\displaystyle\delta
and to obtain
(
b
)
, we made use of the fact that
\displaystyle f
is independent of time along with
Eq.

5.4
.

Applying Young’s inequality in both cases completes the proof.
∎

Proposition 5.12
.

Under the assumption
Eq.

5.4
and
Eq.

5.3
with
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
, there holds

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}+\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}(2\rho)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx.

Proof.

Let us apply
Lemma

5.4
with
\displaystyle\rho_{a}=\rho
and
\displaystyle\rho_{b}=2\rho
, then along with
Lemma

4.9
, we obtain

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz&\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1})}&\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|^{p}\ dz+\lambda^{p-2}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left|\frac{u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)}{2\rho}\right|^{2}\ dz\\
&&+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-\left(u\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{2\rho}$}}}(t)|\ dz.\end{array}

We estimate the right-hand side of the above expression using
Lemma

5.9
,
Lemma

5.10
and
Lemma

5.11
to get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}},

from which we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz+\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{\rho}(x_{0})}\rho^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx&\apprle&\varepsilon\lambda^{p}+C(\varepsilon)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}\\
&&\qquad+\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho}(x_{0})}(2\rho)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx.\end{array}

Now, taking
\displaystyle\varepsilon=\varepsilon(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)\in(0,1)
small enough and making use of
Eq.

5.3
, we obtain the desired estimate.
∎

5.4
Fixing some Constants

Let us fix some constants that will be needed in the covering argument to prove higher integrability.

Definition 5.13
.

Let
\displaystyle r>0
be fixed with
\displaystyle Q_{2r}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
and let
\displaystyle r\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq 2r
be any two radii, then we define the following quantities:

(C1)

For any
\displaystyle\lambda>0
, we denote
\displaystyle E_{\lambda}:=\{z\in Q_{r_{1}}(z_{0}):|\nabla u|+1>\lambda\}
.

(C2)

We define the two constants

\displaystyle\displaystyle d:=\begin{cases}\frac{p}{2}&\text{ if }p\geq 2,\\
\frac{2p}{p(n+2)-2n}&\text{ if }p<2,\end{cases}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\alpha:=\begin{cases}\left(d-\frac{d}{p}\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)^{-1}&\text{ if }p\geq 2,\\
\left(1-\frac{d}{p}\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p}{2}-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)^{-1}&\text{ if }p<2.\end{cases}

(C3)

Fix
\displaystyle\lambda_{0}
such that
\displaystyle\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d}}:=\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|+1\right)^{p}\ dz+\left(\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\alpha}
.

(C4)

We fix a constant
\displaystyle\mathbb{B}
satisfying
\displaystyle\mathbb{B}:=\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{(n+2)\frac{d}{p}}+\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n\frac{d\alpha}{p}}\geq 1
.

(C5)

For
\displaystyle(\mathfrak{x},\mathfrak{t})=\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}
and
\displaystyle\rho\in(0,r_{2}-r_{1})
, we define

G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})):=\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}\left(|\nabla u|+1\right)^{p}\ dz+\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{x})}\rho^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx.

(C6)

We define

\displaystyle\nu:=\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\min\{2,p\}}{2}-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)
and
\displaystyle\tilde{f}(x):=\eta^{-\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\lambda_{0}^{\nu}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f(x)|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}
.

Definition 5.14
.

Let
\displaystyle\eta>0
and
\displaystyle\lambda>1
. For any
\displaystyle\rho\in(0,2r)
, we define the following upper-level sets:

•

\displaystyle\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{\rho}:=\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):(|\nabla u(z)|+1)^{p}>\eta\lambda^{p}\}
,

•

\displaystyle\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{\rho}:=\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):\eta^{-\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\lambda_{0}^{\nu}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f(x)|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}>\eta\lambda^{p}\}=\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):|\tilde{f}|>\eta\lambda^{p}\}
,

where
\displaystyle\tilde{f}
and
\displaystyle\nu
are as defined in
(
C6
)
.

Before we end this subsection, we make an important remark:

Remark 5.15
.

In the rest of the proof of the higher integrability, we will consider the following cylinders:

Case
\displaystyle p\geq 2
:

The cylinder will be of the form
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}:=Q_{r,\lambda^{2-p}r^{2}}
.

Case
\displaystyle p\leq 2
:

The cylinder will be of the form
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}:=Q_{\lambda^{\frac{p-2}{2}}r,r^{2}}
.

By abuse of notation, we shall use the same notation
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}
to denote these two different cylinders and their notation will be clear from the context.

5.5
Covering argument in the case
\displaystyle p\geq 2

Lemma 5.16
.

With the constants fixed as in
Section

5.4
, let
\displaystyle\lambda>2^{\max\left\{\tfrac{d}{p},\tfrac{\alpha d}{p}\right\}}\mathbb{B}\lambda_{0}
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}
, then there exists
\displaystyle\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\in\left(0,\tfrac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\right)
such that the following holds

G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))=\lambda^{p}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\leq\lambda^{p}\ \text{ for all }\ \ \rho\in(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}),

where
\displaystyle G
is defined in
(
C5
)
.

Proof.

From the Lebesgue differentiation theorem, we see that

\lim_{\rho\rightarrow 0}G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\geq(|\nabla u(\mathfrak{z})|+1)^{p}\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C1]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{>}\lambda^{p}.

From the observation
\displaystyle\frac{p}{d\alpha}>0
, we see that for any
\displaystyle\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\leq\rho<r_{2}-r_{1}
, it follows that
\displaystyle Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{2r}(z_{0})
and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))&\leq&\frac{|Q_{2r}(z_{0})|}{|Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})|}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|+1\right)^{p}\ dz+\frac{|B_{2r}(x_{0})|}{|B_{\rho}(x_{0})|}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C3]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C3}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}\lambda^{p-2}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d}}+\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d}\frac{1}{\alpha}}\\
&\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C2]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}},\hyperref@@ii[C4]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C4}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{p}=\lambda^{p}.\end{array}

Let us define
\displaystyle\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\in\left(0,\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\right)
to be the largest value such that
\displaystyle G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))=\lambda^{p}
, this proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma 5.17
.

There exists
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
small such that for any
\displaystyle\lambda>2^{\max\left\{\tfrac{d}{p},\tfrac{\alpha d}{p}\right\}}\mathbb{B}\lambda_{0}
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}
, the following holds:

\iint_{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dz.

(5.8)

Proof.

From
Lemma

5.16
, we see that assumptions
Eq.

5.4
and
Eq.

5.3
are satisfied since
\displaystyle 10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}<r_{2}-r_{1}
. Thus
Proposition

5.12
gives

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle\underbrace{\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}}_{=:I}+\underbrace{\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx}_{=:II}.

(5.9)

In order to estimate
Eq.

5.9
, we will first choose some
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
to be eventually fixed depending on the data and estimate each of the terms as follows:

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Let
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
will be chosen later. Then since
\displaystyle Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0})
holds, we see that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}I&\overset{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lv}}}{\leq}&\eta\lambda^{p}+\left(\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}\\
&\leq&\eta\lambda^{p}+\left(\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}-1}\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{le_la}}{\leq}&\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz.\end{array}

(5.10)

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

To estimate this term, we shall split the proof into two cases as follows (note that
\displaystyle\eta
is yet to be determined):

Case a
: In this case, let us assume that the following holds:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta\lambda^{p}\leq\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx.

(5.11)

Recall that
\displaystyle f=f(x)
, thus we get

\eta\lambda^{p}\leq\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}(\mathfrak{x})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\overset{\text{\hyperref@@ii[C3]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C3}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}}{\leq}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\left(\frac{r}{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}\right)^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d\alpha}}.

(5.12)

It is easy to see that
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}
, since, in the case
\displaystyle p>n
, we trivially have
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}=1
(see
Eq.

5.1
) and in the case
\displaystyle p\leq n
, we have
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\Leftrightarrow\mathfrak{\bf a}>n^{\prime}\Leftrightarrow\delta>\frac{1}{p}
which holds from
Eq.

5.1
.

From
Eq.

5.12
and the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{p}{d\alpha}=p-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)=\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}(p-1)>0
along with
(
C2
)
, we have

\frac{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}{r}\leq\eta^{-\frac{1}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{1}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\left(p-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)}.

(5.13)

Therefore
Eq.

5.13
implies that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx&=&\left(\frac{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}{r}\right)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&\leq&\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\left(p-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\frac{\lambda_{0}^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}}{\eta^{\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{5.37a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\eta^{-\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\lambda_{0}^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\overset{\text{\hyperref@@ii[C6]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C6}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}}{=}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}|\tilde{f}|\ dz\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{5.37b}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx,\end{array}

(5.14)

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we noted that
\displaystyle\left(\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\right)\left(p-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)>0
which follows from
Eq.

5.1
and the restriction
\displaystyle\lambda>\lambda_{0}
; to obtain
(
b
)
, we made use of
Definition

5.14
along with
(
C6
)
.

Case b
: This is the trivial case where
Eq.

5.11
does not hold, i.e., the following holds:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\leq\eta\lambda^{p}.

(5.15)

Combining
Eq.

5.10
and
Eq.

5.14
or
Eq.

5.15
into
Eq.

5.9
, we get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx.

Recalling
(
C5
)
,
Lemma

5.16
and
Eq.

5.9
, we get

G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\apprle\eta G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx.

(5.16)

We now choose
\displaystyle\eta
small enough to absorb
\displaystyle G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))
and recalling the bound
\displaystyle G(Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))<\lambda^{p}=G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))
which holds due to
Lemma

5.16
, we get

\iint_{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dx.

Note that when we multiply
Eq.

5.16
with
\displaystyle|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|
, the last term in
Eq.

5.16
becomes

|I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}|\int_{{}_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx=\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dz.

∎

5.6
Covering argument in the case
\displaystyle p<2

Recall the notation from
Remark

5.15
which will be used in this subsection dealing with the singular case. In particular, we take
\displaystyle\rho=\lambda^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\rho
.

Lemma 5.18
.

With the constants fixed as in
Section

5.4
, let
\displaystyle\lambda>2^{\max\left\{\tfrac{d}{p},\tfrac{\alpha d}{p}\right\}}\mathbb{B}\lambda_{0}
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}
, then there exists
\displaystyle\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\in\left(0,\tfrac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\right)
such that following holds:

G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))=\lambda^{p}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\leq\lambda^{p}\ \text{ for all }\ \ \rho\in(\rho_{\mathfrak{z}},r_{2}-r_{1}),

where
\displaystyle G
is defined in
(
C5
)
.

Proof.

From the Lebesgue differentiation theorem, we see that

\lim_{\rho\rightarrow 0}G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\geq(|\nabla u(\mathfrak{z})|+1)^{p}\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C1]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{>}\lambda^{p}.

From observation
\displaystyle\frac{p}{d\alpha}>0
, we see that for any
\displaystyle\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\leq\rho<r_{2}-r_{1}
, it follows that
\displaystyle Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{2r}(z_{0})
. Recalling that the cylinders are of the form
\displaystyle Q_{\lambda^{\frac{p-2}{2}}R,R^{2}}
, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}G(Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))&\leq&\frac{|Q_{2r}(z_{0})|}{|Q_{\rho}^{\lambda}(z_{0})|}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|+1\right)^{p}\ dz+\frac{|B_{2r}(x_{0})|}{|B_{\rho}^{\lambda}(x_{0})|}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)}\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(2r\lambda^{\frac{p-2}{2}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C3]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C3}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n+2}\lambda^{\frac{(2-p)n}{2}}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p(n+2)-2n}{2}}+\left(\frac{20r}{r_{2}-r_{1}}\right)^{n}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\lambda^{\frac{(2-p)n}{2}}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d}\frac{1}{\alpha}}\\
&\overset{\hyperref@@ii[C2]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}},\hyperref@@ii[C4]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C4}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{p}=\lambda^{p}.\end{array}

Let us define
\displaystyle\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}\in\left(0,\frac{r_{2}-r_{1}}{10}\right)
to be the largest value such that
\displaystyle G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))=\lambda^{p}
. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma 5.19
.

There exists
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
small such that for any
\displaystyle\lambda>2^{\max\left\{\tfrac{d}{p},\tfrac{\alpha d}{p}\right\}}\mathbb{B}\lambda_{0}
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}
, the following holds:

\iint_{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dz.

Proof.

From
Lemma

5.16
, we see that assumptions
Eq.

5.4
and
Eq.

5.3
are satisfied since
\displaystyle 10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}<r_{2}-r_{1}
. Thus
Proposition

5.12
gives

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle\underbrace{\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}}_{=:I}+\underbrace{\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx}_{=:II}.

(5.17)

In order to estimate
Eq.

5.17
, we will first choose some
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
to be eventually fixed depending on the data and estimate each of the terms as follows:

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Let
\displaystyle\eta\in(0,1)
will be chosen later. Then since
\displaystyle Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\subset Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0})
holds, we see that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}I&\overset{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lv}}}{\leq}&\eta\lambda^{p}+\left(\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}}\\
&\leq&\eta\lambda^{p}+\left(\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{q}-1}\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{le_la}}{\leq}&\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz.\end{array}

(5.18)

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

To estimate this term, we shall split the proof into two cases as follows (note that
\displaystyle\eta
is yet to be determined):

Case a
: In this case, let us assume that the following holds:

\eta\lambda^{p}\leq\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx.

Recall that
\displaystyle f=f(x)
, thus we get

\eta\lambda^{p}\leq\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{x})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\overset{\text{\hyperref@@ii[C3]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C3}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}}{\leq}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)+\frac{n(2-p)}{2}}\left(\frac{r}{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}\right)^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\lambda_{0}^{\frac{p}{d\alpha}}.

(5.19)

It is easy to see that
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}
, since in the case
\displaystyle p>n
, we trivially have
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}=1
(see
Eq.

5.1
) and in the case
\displaystyle p\leq n
, we have
\displaystyle n>\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\Leftrightarrow\mathfrak{\bf a}>n^{\prime}\Leftrightarrow\delta>\frac{1}{p}
which holds from
Eq.

5.1
.

We see that
\displaystyle\frac{p}{d\alpha}>0
if and only if
\displaystyle\alpha>0
which is equivalent to
\displaystyle d<\frac{2(\mathfrak{\bf a}-1)}{\mathfrak{\bf a}-2}
. Since we are in the case
\displaystyle p\leq 2
and
\displaystyle n\geq 2
, we see that
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}=\frac{n\delta p}{n-\delta p}
, thus
\displaystyle\alpha>0
is equivalent to

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\frac{2p}{p(n+2)-2n}\leq 2\left(\frac{n\delta p-n+\delta p}{\delta p(n+2)-2n}\right)\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad 0&\leq&n(\delta p-1)+p\left(\delta-\frac{\delta p(n+2)-2n}{p(n+2)-2n}\right)\\
&=&n(\delta p-1)+\frac{2np(1-\delta)}{p(n+2)-2n}.\end{array}

Since we assumed
\displaystyle\delta>\frac{1}{p}
from
Eq.

5.1
, the above quantity is positive. Moreover, we see that

p-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)-\frac{n(2-p)}{2}=\frac{p}{d\alpha},

which is used to rewrite
Eq.

5.19
as

\frac{\tilde{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}}{r}\leq\eta^{-\frac{1}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{p}{d\alpha(n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime})}}.

(5.20)

Therefore
Eq.

5.20
implies that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}&&\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&&=\left(\frac{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}{r}\right)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&&\leq\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{d\alpha}-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\frac{\lambda_{0}^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}}{\eta^{\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\\
&&\overset{\hypertarget{5.37sa}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}\eta^{-\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\lambda_{0}^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\overset{\text{\hyperref@@ii[C6]{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\bf C6}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}}{=}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}|\tilde{f}|\ dz\\
&&\overset{\hypertarget{5.37sb}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx,\end{array}

(5.21)

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we noted that
\displaystyle\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
along with the calculations (recall
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}=\tfrac{n\delta p}{n-\delta p}
)

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\frac{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{d\alpha}-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)>0&\Longleftrightarrow&\frac{1}{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{d}-n\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)\right)>0\\
&\Longleftrightarrow&\frac{n(1-\delta)}{\delta p}>0,\end{array}

and to obtain
(
b
)
, we made use of
Definition

5.14
along with
(
C6
)
.

Case b
: This is the trivial case where
Eq.

5.11
does not hold, i.e., the following holds:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\lambda^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(1-\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\right)+\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}\left(\frac{p-2}{2}\right)}\fint_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}})^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\leq\eta\lambda^{p}.

(5.22)

Combining
Eq.

5.18
and
Eq.

5.21
or
Eq.

5.22
into
Eq.

5.17
, we get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle\eta\lambda^{p}+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx.

Recalling
(
C5
)
,
Lemma

5.18
and
Eq.

5.17
, we get

G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))\apprle\eta G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))+\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\frac{1}{|B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|}\int_{B_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx.

(5.23)

We now choose
\displaystyle\eta
small enough to absorb
\displaystyle G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))
and recalling the bound
\displaystyle G(Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))<\lambda^{p}=G(Q_{\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}))
which holds due to
Lemma

5.18
, we get

\iint_{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dx.

Note that when we multiply
Eq.

5.23
with
\displaystyle|Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})|
, the last term in
Eq.

5.23
becomes

|I_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}|\int_{{}_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx=\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}(x)|\ dz.

∎

5.7
Vitali Covering lemma

Lemma 5.20
.

With
\displaystyle\eta
fixed according to
Lemma

5.17
and
Lemma

5.19
, let
\displaystyle\lambda_{1}=2^{\max\left\{\tfrac{d}{p},\tfrac{\alpha d}{p}\right\}}\mathbb{B}\lambda_{0}
, then for any
\displaystyle\lambda>\lambda_{1}
, there holds

\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta)}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz.

(5.24)

Proof.

Applying Vitali covering Lemma to
\displaystyle\{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\}_{\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}}
where the cylinders are as obtained in
Lemma

5.16
and
Lemma

5.18
, then we obtain disjoint countable subfamily
\displaystyle\{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}_{i})\}_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}\in E_{\lambda}}
such that

E_{\lambda}\subset\bigcup_{\mathfrak{z}\in E_{\lambda}}Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z})\subset\bigcup_{1\leq i<\infty}Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}_{i})\subset Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0}),

where the last inclusion holds since
\displaystyle 10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}\leq r_{2}-r_{1}
. Thus we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\iint_{E_{\lambda}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz&\leq&\sum_{1\leq i<\infty}\iint_{Q_{10\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}_{i})}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{step1_est}}{\apprle}&\sum_{1\leq i<\infty}\left(\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}_{i})\cap\Phi^{r_{2}}_{\eta\lambda}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{Q_{2\rho_{\mathfrak{z}_{i}}^{\lambda}(\mathfrak{z}_{i})\cap\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz\right)\\
&\leq&\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz.\end{array}

Also, since there holds

\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{1}}\setminus E_{\lambda}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\leq\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{1}}\setminus E_{\lambda}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz,

from which it follows that

\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz=\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{1}}\setminus E_{\lambda}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz+\iint_{E_{\lambda}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\apprle\iint_{\Phi_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz+\iint_{\Sigma_{\eta\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz.

∎

5.8
Proof of
Theorem

2.2

For
\displaystyle k>\lambda_{1}
, let us define

\displaystyle\displaystyle|\nabla u|_{k}:=\min\{|\nabla u|+1,k\}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\Phi^{\rho}_{\lambda,k}:=\{z\in Q_{\rho}(z_{0}):|\nabla u|^{p}_{k}>\lambda^{p}\}.

From this, we see that for
\displaystyle\lambda>k
, then
\displaystyle\Phi^{r_{1}}_{\lambda,k}=\varnothing
and if
\displaystyle\lambda\leq k
, then
\displaystyle\Phi^{r_{1}}_{\lambda,k}=\Phi^{r_{1}}_{\lambda}
and
\displaystyle\Phi^{r_{2}}_{\lambda,k}=\Phi^{r_{2}}_{\lambda}
. Thus from
Eq.

5.24
, we deduce

\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda,k}^{r_{1}}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\apprle\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda,k}^{r_{2}}}\lambda^{p-q}|\nabla u|^{q}\ dz+\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz.

(5.25)

Let
\displaystyle a>0
. Multiplying
Eq.

5.25
with
\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}
and integrating over
\displaystyle(\lambda_{1},\infty)
, we get

\underbrace{\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda,k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\ d\lambda}_{=:I}\apprle\underbrace{\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{p-q+a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda,k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\ dz\ d\lambda}_{=:II}+\underbrace{\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dz\ d\lambda}_{=:III}.

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Apply Fubini to get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}I=\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\ d\lambda\ dz&\geq&\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz\\
&&-\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log(e+\lambda_{1}^{a})\ dz.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

Fubini gives

\begin{array}[]{rclll}II=\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}\frac{\lambda^{p-q+a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\ d\lambda\ dz&\leq&\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}{\lambda^{p-q-1}}\ d\lambda\ dz\\
&\leq&\frac{1}{p-q}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle III
:

Fubini implies

III=\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda_{1}}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\tilde{f}|}\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\ d\lambda\ dz\leq\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda_{1}}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\log(e+|\tilde{f}|^{a})\ dz.

Combining the above estimates, we get

\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1},\delta,a)}\log(e+\lambda_{1}^{a})\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz+\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda_{1}}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\log(e+|\tilde{f}|^{a})\ dz.

On the other hand, we trivially have

\iint_{\{z\in Q_{r_{1}}(z_{0}):|\nabla u|_{k}\leq\lambda_{1}\}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz\leq\log(e+\lambda_{1}^{a})\iint_{\{z\in Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0}):|\nabla u|_{k}\leq\lambda_{1}\}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz,

which gives

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{1}}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz\apprle\log(e+\lambda_{0}^{d})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{2}}(z_{0})}|\tilde{f}|\log(e+|\tilde{f}|)\ dz.

Take
\displaystyle r_{1}=r
and
\displaystyle r_{2}=2r
and let
\displaystyle k\to\infty
, which completes the proof.

6
Proof of
Corollary

2.3

Let
\displaystyle a>0
and
\displaystyle m\in\mathbb{N}
be a given integer, then in
Eq.

5.25
, we multiply with
\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\log_{(m-1)}(e+\lambda^{a})
and integrate to get

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Apply Fubini to get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}I&=&\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\log_{(m-1)}(e+\lambda^{a})\ d\lambda\ dz\\
&\geq&\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log_{(m)}(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz\\
&&-\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{1}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log_{(m)}(e+\lambda_{1}^{a})\ dz.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

Fubini gives

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II&=&\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}\log_{(m-1)}(e+\lambda^{a})\frac{\lambda^{p-q+a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\ d\lambda\ dz\\
&\leq&\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{q}\log_{(m-1)}(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\nabla u|_{k}}{\lambda^{p-q-1}}\ d\lambda\ dz\\
&\leq&\frac{1}{p-q}\iint_{\Phi_{\lambda_{1},k}^{r_{2}}}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log_{(m-1)}(e+|\nabla u|_{k}^{a})\ dz.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle III
:

Fubini implies

III=\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda_{1}}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\int_{\lambda_{1}}^{|\tilde{f}|}\log_{(m-1)}(e+\lambda^{a})\frac{\lambda^{a-1}}{e+\lambda^{a}}\ d\lambda\ dz\leq\frac{1}{a}\iint_{\Sigma_{\lambda_{1}}^{r_{2}}}|\tilde{f}|\log_{(m)}(e+|\tilde{f}|^{a})\ dz.

Proceeding as in
Section

5.8
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log_{(m)}(e+|\nabla u|^{a})\ dz&\apprle&\log_{(m)}(e+\lambda_{0}^{d})\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz\\
&&+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}|\tilde{f}|\log_{(m)}(e+|\tilde{f}|)\ dz\\
&&+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\log_{(m-1)}(e+|\nabla u|^{a})\ dz.\end{array}

Iterating the estimate over
\displaystyle m=\{0,1,\ldots,m_{0}\}
proves the Corollary.

7
Proof of
Corollary

2.5

Given
\displaystyle\delta\in(0,1)
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}=\frac{n\delta p}{n-\delta p}
, then there exists
\displaystyle\varepsilon_{1}=\varepsilon_{1}(p,\delta)
such that for any
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{1})
, there holds

\frac{p}{p-1}+\frac{\varepsilon}{p-1}<(\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime})^{*}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad\varepsilon<\frac{p(1-\delta)}{p\delta-1}.

Applying
Lemma

3.7
for a.e.
\displaystyle t\in I_{2r}(t_{0})
, it follows that

\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}|I_{1}g(x,t)|^{\frac{p}{p-1}+\frac{\varepsilon}{p-1}}\ dx\apprle_{(n,p,\delta)}(2r)^{\alpha}\left(\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|g(x,t)|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{(p-1)\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{(p-1)\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}},

where

\alpha=\left(\frac{p+\varepsilon}{p-1}\right)\left(\frac{n\gamma}{s}+\frac{n}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}-1\right)-n\quad\text{with}\ \gamma=\frac{(1-n)s+n}{s}\ \text{and}\ s=\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}+\frac{p-1}{p+\varepsilon}}.

With these choices, it is easy to see that
\displaystyle\alpha=0
, which immediately gives

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}|I_{1}g|^{\frac{p}{p-1}+\frac{\varepsilon}{p-1}}\ dx\apprle\fint_{I_{2r}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(2r)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|g|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{(p-1)\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}+\frac{\varepsilon}{(p-1)\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\ dt.

(7.1)

On the other hand,
Theorem

2.4
gives that there exists a
\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}=\varepsilon_{2}(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1})
such that for any
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{2})
there holds

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p+\varepsilon}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1})}\lambda_{0}^{\varepsilon}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}(|\nabla u|+1)^{p}\ dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2r}(z_{0})}|I_{1}g|^{\frac{p}{p-1}+\frac{\varepsilon}{p-1}}\ dx.

(7.2)

Hence, taking
\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}=\min\{\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}\}
and combining
Eq.

7.1
and
Eq.

7.2
, this completes the proof.

8
Proof of
Theorem

2.7
- Borderline gradient potential estimates

In this section, we consider a weak solution
\displaystyle u\in C(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))\cap L^{p}(0,T;W^{1,p}(\Omega))
of

u_{t}-\dv|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u=f(x)\text{ in }\Omega_{T}.

(8.1)

8.1
Fixing some quantities

Definition 8.1
.

Let us define the following constants:

(C7)

We denote
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}:=\frac{np}{n-p}
if
\displaystyle p<n
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}:=\infty
if
\displaystyle p>n
.

(C8)

For
\displaystyle p\neq n
and
\displaystyle\mathfrak{\bf a}
defined above, we denote

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{p}^{f}(B_{r}(x_{0})):=\int_{0}^{r}\left[\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{s}(x_{0}))}{s^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}(p-1)}}\ \frac{ds}{s}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\mathbf{P}^{f}_{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{r}(x_{0})):=\int_{0}^{r}\left(\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{s}(x_{0}))}{s^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\ \frac{ds}{s}.

(C9)

In the case
\displaystyle p=n
, we shall denote

|f|_{L\log L(B_{s}(x_{0}))}:=\int_{B_{s}(x_{0})}|f|\log\left(e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{s}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)\ dx,

and

\mathbf{M}_{n}^{f}(B_{r}(x_{0})):=\int_{0}^{r}\left[s^{-(n-1)}|f|_{L\log L(B_{s}(x_{0}))}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}\ \frac{ds}{s},

where the norm
\displaystyle L\log L
is defined in
Eq.

3.2
.

(C10)

We define

\mathbf{F}=\mathbf{F}(B_{2\rho}):=\begin{cases}\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}^{f}(B_{2\rho})&\text{ if }p<2,\\
\mathbf{M}_{p}^{f}(B_{2\rho})&\text{ if }p\geq 2.\end{cases}

Definition 8.2
.

Let
\displaystyle q\in[1,\infty)
, then for any measurable set
\displaystyle Q
and integrable function
\displaystyle h
defined in
\displaystyle Q
, we define excess functional

E_{q}(h,Q):=\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q}|h-(h)_{Q}|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}.

Remark 8.3
.

Note that for any
\displaystyle\Gamma\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, there holds that

E_{q}(h,Q)\leq 2\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q}|h-\Gamma|^{q}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}.

(8.2)

The proof of the following lemma can be found in
[
9
, Equation (85)]
.

Lemma 8.4
.

Let
\displaystyle p\neq n
,
\displaystyle\sigma\in(0,1)
and
\displaystyle r_{j}:=\sigma^{j}r
. Then there holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j<\infty}\left[\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{r_{j}}(x_{0}))}{r_{j}^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}(p-1)}}\apprle_{(n,p,\sigma)}\mathbf{M}_{p}^{f}(B_{r}(x_{0}))\qquad\text{and}\qquad\sum_{1\leq j<\infty}\left[\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{r_{j}}(x_{0}))}{r_{j}^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\apprle_{(n,\sigma)}\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}^{f}(B_{r}(x_{0})).

Similar calculations as in the proof of
Lemma

8.4
give the following lemma:

Lemma 8.5
.

Let
\displaystyle p=n
,
\displaystyle\sigma\in(0,1)
and
\displaystyle r_{j}:=\sigma^{j}r
. Then there holds that

\sum_{1\leq j<\infty}\left[r_{j}^{-(n-1)}|f|_{L\log L(B_{r_{j}}(x_{0}))}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}\apprle_{(\sigma)}\int_{0}^{r}\left[s^{-(n-1)}|f|_{L\log L(B_{s}(x_{0}))}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}\ \frac{ds}{s}.

Proof.

For
\displaystyle r\in[r_{j},r_{j-1}]
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|f|_{L\log L(B_{r_{j}}(x_{0}))}&=&\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\log\left(\frac{e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}}{e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}}\left(e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)\right)\ dx\\
&\leq&\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\log\left(e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)+|f|\log\left(1+\frac{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)\ dx,\end{array}

where to obtain the last inequality, we made use of the bound
\displaystyle\frac{e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}}{e+\frac{|f|}{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}}\leq 1+\frac{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}
.

Moreover, we see that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\log\left(1+\frac{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)\ dx&\leq&\frac{\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}{\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\int_{B_{r_{j}}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx\\
&\leq&\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx\\
&\leq&|f|_{L\log L(B_{r}(x_{0}))}.\end{array}

Therefore we obtain

\left[r_{j}^{-(n-1)}|f|_{L\log L(B_{r_{j}}(x_{0}))}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}\leq\frac{\log\tfrac{1}{\sigma}}{\sigma}\int_{r_{j}}^{r_{j-1}}\left[\frac{|f|_{L\log L(B_{r}(x_{0}))}}{r^{n-1}}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}\frac{dr}{r}.

∎

Definition 8.6
.

Let
\displaystyle\rho
be fixed and for
\displaystyle Q_{2\rho}\subset\Omega_{T}
, we assume that there exist
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{1},\mathbf{H}_{2}
and
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
such that

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{\max\{\frac{p}{p-1},p\}}=\lambda^{p}.

(8.3)

These constants will eventually be chosen appropriately in
Section

8.5
and
Section

8.6
.

We will need the following simple estimate:

Lemma 8.7
.

Suppose
\displaystyle p<2
and
Eq.

8.3
hold. Let
\displaystyle z_{0}\in Q_{2r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})\subset Q_{2\rho}
, then we have

\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}(r|f|)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\apprle_{(n,p)}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{2}}\right)^{p-1}.

Proof.

There exists
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\displaystyle 2^{-(k+1)}\rho\leq r\leq 2^{-k}\rho
. Applying
Lemma

8.4
to get

\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}(r|f|)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\apprle_{(n)}\left(\fint_{B_{2^{-k}\rho}(x_{0})}((2^{-k}\rho)|f|)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\overset{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{p_sum}}}{\apprle}\mathbf{F}\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{p_la}}{\leq}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{2}}\right)^{p-1}.

This completes the proof.
∎

8.2
Regularity for the homogeneous equation

Let us enumerate Lemmas will be used in this section. First of all, fundamental regularity results obtained by DiBenedetto in
[
8
, Chapter VIII-IX]
. More specifically, the Lipschitz estimate is from
[
8
, Theorems 5.1 and 5.2 of Chapter VIII]
and the oscillation decay estimate can be found in
[
8
, Section 3 of Chapter IX]
.

Lemma 8.8
.

Let
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
and
\displaystyle v\in L^{p}(I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0}),W^{1,p}(B_{r}(x_{0})))
be a weak solution of

v_{t}-\dv|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v=0\text{ in }Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}).

Suppose there exists
\displaystyle c_{*}\geq 1
such that

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\leq c_{*}\lambda^{p}.

Then there holds that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{\frac{1}{2}Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla v|<c\lambda\qquad\text{and}\qquad\osc\limits\limits_{sQ_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\langle\nabla v\ ,e_{i}\rangle\leq cs^{\alpha}\lambda,

where
\displaystyle e_{i}=(0,\ldots,0,\underbrace{1}_{i^{th}place},0,\ldots,0)
with
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq n
,
\displaystyle c=c(n,p,c_{*})
,
\displaystyle\alpha=\alpha(n,p,c_{*})\in(0,1)
and
\displaystyle s\in(0,\tfrac{1}{2})
.

The proof of the following decay estimate can be found in
[
14
, Theorem 3.1]
.

Lemma 8.9
.

Let
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
and
\displaystyle v\in L^{p}(I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0}),W^{1,p}(B_{r}(x_{0})))
be a weak solution of

v_{t}-\dv|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v=0\text{ in }Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}).

For any
\displaystyle q\in[1,\infty)
, consider
\displaystyle A,B\geq 1
and
\displaystyle\gamma\in(0,1)
. Then there exists
\displaystyle\sigma_{0}=\sigma_{0}(n,p,A,B,\gamma)
such that if

\frac{\lambda}{B}\leq\sup_{\sigma_{0}Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla v|\leq\sup_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla v|\leq A\lambda,

(8.4)

holds, then for any
\displaystyle\sigma\in(0,\sigma_{0})
, the following holds:

E_{q}(\nabla v,\sigma Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}))\leq\gamma E_{q}(\nabla v,Q_{r}^{\lambda}).

8.3
Comparison estimate when
\displaystyle p\neq n

Consider
\displaystyle v\in C(I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0});W^{1,p}(B_{r}(x_{0})))
be a weak solution of

\begin{cases}v_{t}-\dv|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v=0&\text{ in }Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}),\\
v=u&\text{ on }\partial_{p}Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}).\end{cases}

(8.5)

Then we have the following estimate:

Lemma 8.10
.

Let
\displaystyle u
be a weak solution of
Eq.

8.1
and
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
and
Eq.

8.3
hold. For weak solution
\displaystyle v
of
Eq.

8.5
if
\displaystyle p\geq 2
and
\displaystyle p\neq n
, then there holds

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p)}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{p-1}},

(8.6)

and if
\displaystyle p<2
, then there holds

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\apprle_{(n,p)}\left(\fint_{B_{r}^{\lambda}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{p-1}}+\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{(2-p)p}\left(\fint_{B_{r}^{\lambda}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}.

(8.7)

Proof.

The proof is analogue to
[
9
, Lemma 5]
for the case
\displaystyle p\geq 2
and
\displaystyle p\neq n
and to
[
18
, Lemma 3.3]
for the case
\displaystyle p<2
. For the sake of completeness, we present all the details.

We make use of
\displaystyle u-v
as a test function for
\displaystyle(u-v)_{t}-\dv\left(|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u-|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v\right)=g
. Then, we obtain
