\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\partial_{t}(u-v)^{2}\ dz+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\langle|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u-|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v\ ,\nabla u-\nabla v\rangle\ dz}_{=:I}\leq\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-v|\ dz}_{=:II}.\end{array}

Note that
\displaystyle u-v=0
at the bottom of the cylinder
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})
and hence we can ignore the contribution arising from this term.

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Applying
Lemma

4.3
, we get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p-2}{2}}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{2}\ dz\apprle I.

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

To estimate this term, we further consider the following subcases:

Case
\displaystyle 1<p<n
:

In this case, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II&\leq&\fint_{I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\left|\frac{u-v}{r}\right|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}}}\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{7.5a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\apprle}&\fint_{I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{7.5b}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&C(\gamma)\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{p-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz,\end{array}

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we used Poincar
\displaystyle\acute{\text{e}}
inequality and to obtain
(
b
)
, we used Young’s inequality.

Case
\displaystyle p>n
:

In this case, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II&\leq&\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx\right)\fint_{I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\sup_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|u(\cdot,t)-v(\cdot,t)|\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{7.7a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\apprle}&\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r|f|\ dx\right)\fint_{I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0})}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\ dt\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{7.7b}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&C(\gamma)\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r|f|\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz,\end{array}

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we used
Lemma

4.6
and to obtain
(
b
)
, we used Young’s inequality.

We now combine the previous estimates to prove the lemma as follows:

Proof of
Eq.

8.6
:

Applying
Lemma

4.3
, we obtain

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\apprle C(\gamma)\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{p-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz.

Choosing
\displaystyle\gamma
suitably small gives the desired estimate.

Proof of
Eq.

8.7
:

Lemma

4.3
implies

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz&&\apprle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p-2}{2}}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{2}\ dz\\
&&\quad+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p-2}{2}}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{2}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{2-p}{2}}\\
&&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{p_la}}{\leq}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p-2}{2}}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{2}\ dz\\
&&\qquad+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\left(|\nabla u|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\right)^{\frac{p-2}{2}}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{2}\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{\frac{(2-p)p}{2}},\end{array}

and thus, Young’s inequality gives

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz&\leq&C(\gamma)\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r|f|\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{p-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\\
&&+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-v|\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{\frac{(2-p)p}{2}}.\end{array}

(8.8)

In order to estimate the last term, we proceed as follows:

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|f||u-v|\ dz\right)^{\frac{p}{2}}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{\frac{(2-p)p}{2}}\\
\leq\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{2}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{\frac{(2-p)p}{2}}\\
\overset{\hypertarget{7.6a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}C(\gamma)\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{(2-p)p}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz.

(8.9)

Combining
Eq.

8.8
and
Eq.

8.9
followed by choosing
\displaystyle\gamma
small gives the desired estimate.

This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Corollary 8.11
.

In the case
\displaystyle 1<p\leq 2
under the hypothesis of
Lemma

8.10
, we have

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\apprle\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{2-p}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\left(r|f|\right)^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}.

Proof.

Let us estimate the first term in
Eq.

8.7
. Since
\displaystyle p\leq 2
, we have
\displaystyle p\leq p^{\prime}
which gives

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{p}{p-1}}&=&\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\frac{(2-p)p}{p-1}}\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{7.24a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\left(\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\right)^{(2-p)p}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}\ dx\right)^{\frac{p}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}},\end{array}

where to obtain
(
a
)
, we used
Lemma

8.7
. This completes the proof of the corollary.
∎

8.4
Comparison estimate when
\displaystyle p=n

Consider the weak solution
\displaystyle v\in C(I_{r}^{\lambda}(t_{0});W^{1,p}(B_{r}(x_{0})))
of

\begin{cases}v_{t}-\dv|\nabla v|^{p-2}\nabla v=0&\text{ in }Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}),\\
v=u&\text{ on }\partial_{p}Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0}).\end{cases}

(8.10)

Lemma 8.12
.

Let
\displaystyle u
be a weak solution of
Eq.

8.1
,
\displaystyle Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})\subset\Omega_{T}
and
Eq.

8.3
hold. Suppose that
\displaystyle p=n
, then there exists
\displaystyle r_{0}=r_{0}(n)
such that for any
\displaystyle r\in(0,r_{0})
and for weak solution
\displaystyle v
of
Eq.

8.10
, the following holds

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\nabla u-\nabla v|^{n}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}\apprle_{(n)}\left(\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}r|f|\log\left(e+\frac{|f|}{\fint_{B_{r}(x_{0})}|f|\ dx}\right)\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n-1}}.

Proof.

We will prove the lemma at
\displaystyle r_{0}
which will be determined according to
Eq.

8.11
. For
\displaystyle(x,t)\in Q_{r_{0}}
, we define

\tilde{u}(x,t):=\lambda^{-1}u\left(x_{0}+x,t_{0}+\lambda^{2-p}t\right)\qquad\text{and}\qquad\tilde{f}(x):=\lambda^{1-p}f\left(x_{0}+x\right).

Then
\displaystyle\tilde{u}\in C(I_{r_{0}};L^{2}(B_{r_{0}}(x_{0})))\cap L^{p}(I_{r_{0}};W^{1,p}(B_{r_{0}}))
is a weak solution of

\tilde{u}_{t}-\dv|\nabla\tilde{u}|^{p-2}\nabla\tilde{u}=\tilde{f}\text{ in }Q_{r_{0}}.

Let
\displaystyle\tilde{v}\in C(I_{r_{0}};L^{2}(B_{r_{0}}))\cap L^{n}(I_{r_{0}};W^{1,n}(B_{r_{0}}))
be the weak solution of

\begin{cases}\tilde{v}_{t}-\dv|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n-2}\nabla\tilde{v}=0&\text{ in }Q_{r_{0}},\\
\tilde{v}=\tilde{u}&\text{ on }\partial_{p}Q_{r_{0}}.\end{cases}

Using
\displaystyle\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}
as a test function for
\displaystyle(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v})_{t}-\dv\left(|\nabla\tilde{u}|^{p-2}\nabla\tilde{u}-|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p-2}\nabla\tilde{v}\right)=\tilde{f}
, the standard energy estimate gives the following inequality

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\partial_{t}(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v})^{2}\ dz+\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}\langle|\nabla\tilde{u}|^{p-2}\nabla\tilde{u}-|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{p-2}\nabla\tilde{v}\ ,\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}\rangle\ dz}_{=:I}\leq\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r}^{\lambda}(z_{0})}|\tilde{f}||\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}|\ dz}_{=:II}.

Estimate of
\displaystyle I
:

Applying
Lemma

4.3
, we get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz\apprle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}\left(|\nabla\tilde{u}|^{2}+|\nabla\tilde{v}|^{2}\right)^{\frac{n-2}{2}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{2}\ dz\apprle_{(n)}II.

Estimate of
\displaystyle II
:

We will split this integral into two parts as follow:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II\leq\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\tilde{f}||\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|\ dz+\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\tilde{f}||\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|\ dz:=II_{1}+II_{2}.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle II_{1}
:

Applying
Lemma

3.5
with
\displaystyle\Phi(s)=(1+s)\log(1+s)-s
and
\displaystyle\tilde{\Phi}(s)=\exp(s)-s-1
, we get

II_{1}\apprle\fint_{I_{r_{0}}}\frac{1}{|B_{r_{0}}|}\lVert\tilde{f}\rVert_{L^{\Phi}(B_{r_{0}})}\lVert\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}\rVert_{L^{\tilde{\Phi}}(B_{r_{0}})}\ dt.

Note that for all
\displaystyle s>0
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\int_{B_{r_{0}}}\tilde{\Phi}\left(\frac{|\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|}{s}\right)\ dx&\leq&\int_{B_{r_{0}}}\exp\left(\frac{|\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|}{s}\right)\ dx,\end{array}

and
\displaystyle(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v})(\cdot,t)\in W^{1,n}_{0}(B_{r_{0}})
satisfies the hypothesis of
Theorem

4.7
with
\displaystyle M=\left(\int_{B_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}
. Thus, taking
\displaystyle\sigma=\sigma(n)=\frac{1}{2^{n}}w_{n}\sigma_{0}
in
Theorem

4.7
, we get

\int_{B_{r_{0}}}\exp\left(\frac{\sigma|\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|}{\left(\int_{B_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}}\right)\ dx\leq c(n)r_{0}^{n}.

(8.11)

Now, we choose
\displaystyle r_{0}=r_{0}(n)
small such that
\displaystyle c(n)r_{0}^{n}\leq 1
where
\displaystyle c(n)
is the constant in
Eq.

8.11
. This implies

\frac{1}{\sigma}\left(\int_{B_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}\leq s\Longrightarrow\int_{B_{r_{0}}}\tilde{\Phi}\left(\frac{|\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}|}{s}\right)\ dx\leq 1,

and thus we have

\lVert\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}-\left(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v}\right)_{\mathchoice{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\textstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}{\hskip-1.0pt\raisebox{-1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle B_{r_{0}}$}}}\rVert_{L^{\tilde{\Phi}}(B_{r_{0}})}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma}\left(\int_{B_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}.

Therefore, applying Young’s inequality, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II_{1}&\apprle&r_{0}^{-(n-1)}\lVert\tilde{f}\rVert_{L^{\Phi}(B_{r_{0}})}\fint_{I_{r_{0}}}\left(\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dx\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}\ dt\\
&\leq&C(\gamma)\left(r_{0}^{-(n-1)}\lVert\tilde{f}\rVert_{L^{\Phi}(B_{r_{0}})}\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz.\end{array}

Estimate of
\displaystyle II_{2}
:

Applying Poincar
\displaystyle\acute{\text{e}}
inequality in the spatial direction followed by Young’s inequality, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}II_{2}&=&\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}|\tilde{f}|\ dx\fint_{I_{r_{0}}}|(\tilde{u}-\tilde{v})_{B_{r_{0}}}|\ dt\\
&\apprle&\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}r_{0}|\tilde{f}|\ dx\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{n}}\\
&\leq&C(\gamma)\left(\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}r_{0}|\tilde{f}|\ dx\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}+\gamma\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz.\end{array}

Therefore taking
\displaystyle\gamma=\gamma(n)\in(0,1)
small enough, we obtain

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz\apprle\left(r_{0}^{-(n-1)}\lVert\tilde{f}\rVert_{L^{\Phi}(B_{r_{0}})}\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}+\left(\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}r_{0}|\tilde{f}|\ dx\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}.

And we apply
Eq.

3.3
to get

\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{r_{0}}}|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|^{n}\ dz\apprle\left(\fint_{B_{r_{0}}}r_{0}|\tilde{f}|\log\left(e+\frac{|\tilde{f}|}{\lVert\tilde{f}\rVert_{L^{1}(B_{r_{0}})}}\right)\ dx\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}.

Scaling back from
\displaystyle|\nabla\tilde{u}-\nabla\tilde{v}|
to
\displaystyle\nabla u-\nabla v|
gives the required estimate and this proves the lemma.
∎

8.5
Decay estimate

Let us first fix some notation:

•

Let
\displaystyle z_{0}\in Q_{\rho}
be a Lebesgue point of
\displaystyle\nabla u
and
\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathbf{F}_{j}(z_{0})<\infty
,

•

\displaystyle\sigma=\sigma(n,p)\in(0,1/4)
will be determined in
Eq.

8.22
,

•

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{1}
depending on
\displaystyle\sigma,n
and
\displaystyle p
will be determined according to
Eq.

8.12
,

•

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}
depending on
\displaystyle\sigma,n
and
\displaystyle p
will be determined according to
Eq.

8.19
and
Eq.

8.23
,

•

For
\displaystyle j\geq 1
, we denote
\displaystyle\rho_{j}:=\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{j-1}\rho
,
\displaystyle Q_{j}=Q_{\rho_{j}}^{\lambda}(z_{0})
and
\displaystyle B_{j}=B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0})
,

•

We take

\mathbf{F}_{j}(z_{0})=\mathbf{F}_{j}:=\begin{cases}\left[\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0}))}{\rho_{j}^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}&\text{ if }p<2,\\
\left[\frac{|f|^{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}(B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0}))}{\rho_{j}^{n-\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}}}\right]^{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{\bf a}^{\prime}(p-1)}}&\text{ if }p\geq 2\text{ and }p\neq n,\\
\left[\rho_{j}^{-(n-1)}|f|_{L\log L(B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0}))}\right]^{\frac{1}{n-1}}&\text{ if }p=n.\end{cases}

Let us define

G_{j}:=\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j}).

From
Eq.

8.3
and
Eq.

8.2
, we see that

G_{1}\leq 3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{1}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq 3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}2^{\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq 3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}2^{\frac{n+2}{p}}\frac{1}{\mathbf{H}_{1}}\lambda.

Therefore taking
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{1}=\mathbf{H}_{1}(\sigma,n,p)
, we may assume the following : There exists
\displaystyle j_{0}\geq 1
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle G_{j_{0}}\leq\frac{1}{64}\lambda\qquad\text{ and }\qquad G_{j}>\frac{1}{64}\lambda\text{ for }j>j_{0}.

(8.12)

If
Eq.

8.12
does not hold, then this trivially implies
\displaystyle\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}G_{j}\leq\tfrac{1}{64}\lambda
which proves
Theorem

2.7
after making use of
Definition

8.6
.

In this subsection, we will prove the following proposition.

Proposition 8.13
.

Let
\displaystyle u
be a weak solution of
Eq.

8.1
. Suppose
Eq.

8.3
holds. There exist
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{1}\geq 1
and
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}\geq 1
depending on
\displaystyle n
and
\displaystyle p
such that if
Eq.

8.3
holds for some
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
, then

|\nabla u(z_{0})|\apprle\lambda.

The proof follows by induction argument applied to
8.14
. Thus let us prove the following claim:

Claim 8.14
.

Suppose there exists
\displaystyle j_{1}>j_{0}
such that for all
\displaystyle j_{0}\leq j\leq j_{1}
, the following is satisfied:

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j})\leq\lambda.

(8.13)

Then, the following holds for
\displaystyle j_{1}+1
:

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j_{1}+1}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j_{1}+1})\leq\lambda.

(8.14)

Proof of
8.14
.

The proof proceeds in two steps.

STEP 1:

Let
\displaystyle j_{0}\leq j\leq j_{1}
and
\displaystyle v_{j}\in C(I_{\rho_{j}}^{\lambda}(t_{0});L^{2}(B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0})))\cap L^{p}(I_{\rho_{j}}^{\lambda}(t_{0});W^{1,p}(B_{\rho_{j}}(x_{0})))
be the weak solution of

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}v_{j}-\dv|\nabla v_{j}|^{p-2}\nabla v_{j}=0&\text{ in }Q_{j},\\
v_{j}=u&\text{ on }\partial_{p}Q_{j}.\end{cases}

In this step, we want to apply
Lemma

8.9
which requires verifying the hypothesis in
Eq.

8.4
.

Applying
Lemma

8.10
when
\displaystyle p\geq 2
or
Corollary

8.11
when
\displaystyle 1<p\leq 2
and
Lemma

8.12
along with
Eq.

8.3
and
Lemma

8.4
, we deduce that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\apprle_{(n,p)}\lambda^{\max\{0,2-p\}}\mathbf{F}_{j}&\leq&\lambda^{\max\{0,2-p\}}\sum_{1\leq j<\infty}\mathbf{F}_{j}\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{p_la}}{\leq}&\mathbf{C}\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{2}^{\min\{p-1,1\}}}.\end{array}

(8.15)

Noting that
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}\geq 1
, combining
Eq.

8.13
and
Eq.

8.15
gives

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\apprle_{(n,p)}\lambda,

and thus
Lemma

8.8
implies that there exists
\displaystyle A=A(n,p)
and
\displaystyle\alpha=\alpha(n,p)\in(0,1)
such that for any
\displaystyle s\in(0,1)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{\frac{1}{2}Q_{j}}|\nabla v_{j}|\leq A\lambda\qquad\text{and}\qquad\osc\limits\limits_{\frac{s}{2}Q_{j}}\nabla v_{j}\leq As^{\alpha}\lambda.

(8.16)

This proves the upper bound of the hypothesis
Eq.

8.4
holds.

We now prove the lower bound of the hypothesis
Eq.

8.4
holds. Let
\displaystyle 1<k\in\mathbb{N}
to be determined later, then the following sequence of estimates hold:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\frac{1}{64}\lambda\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{km}}{<}G_{j+k}&\overset{\hypertarget{ind1_a}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}a}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\\
&&+2\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla u-(\nabla v_{j})_{Q_{j+k}}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\\
&\overset{\hypertarget{ind1_b}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(}{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}b}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0})}}}{\leq}&\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\\
&&\qquad\qquad\qquad+2\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}-(\nabla v_{j})_{Q_{j+k}}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}},\end{array}

(8.17)

where to obtain
(
a
)
and
(
b
)
, we used triangle inequality for
\displaystyle\|\cdot\|_{p}
.
Note that the following bounds hold:

2\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}-(\nabla v_{j})_{Q_{j+k}}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{j_osc}}{\leq}2A\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}+\alpha(k-1)}\lambda\leq 2A\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}+\alpha(k-1)}\lambda\leq\frac{1}{256}\lambda,

(8.18)

and

3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{ind_diff}}{\leq}3\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}(k+1)}\mathbf{C}\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{2}^{\min\{p-1,1\}}}\lambda\leq\frac{1}{256}\lambda,

(8.19)

provided
\displaystyle k=k(n,p)>1+\frac{n+2}{\alpha p}
and
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}(\sigma,n,p)
are large enough.

Combining
Eq.

8.18
and
Eq.

8.19
into
Eq.

8.17
, we have

\frac{1}{128}\lambda\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\sup_{Q_{j+k}}|\nabla v_{j}|\leq\sup_{Q_{j+1}}|\nabla v_{j}|.

This shows that
Eq.

8.4
is satisfied, which says that for any
\displaystyle\gamma\in(0,1)
and
\displaystyle\sigma=\sigma(\gamma,n,p)
, the following conclusion holds:

E_{p}(\nabla v_{j},Q_{j+1})\leq\gamma E_{p}(\nabla v_{j},\tfrac{1}{2}Q_{j}).

(8.20)

STEP 2:

In this step, we will prove that the induction conclusion
Eq.

8.14
holds. In order to do this, let us first estimate the decay of
\displaystyle E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j})
as follows:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j+1})&\leq&2\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+1}}|\nabla u-(\nabla v_{j})_{Q_{j+1}}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\\
&\leq&2\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+1}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+2E_{p}(\nabla v_{j},Q_{j+1})\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{hom_decay}}{\leq}&2\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+2\gamma E_{p}(\nabla v_{j},\tfrac{1}{2}Q_{j}).\end{array}

Similarly, there holds

\begin{array}[]{rcl}E_{p}(\nabla v_{j},\tfrac{1}{2}Q_{j})&\leq&2^{1+\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+2E_{p}(\nabla u,\tfrac{1}{2}Q_{j})\\
&\leq&2^{1+\frac{n+2}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j}}|\nabla u-\nabla v_{j}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+2^{2+\frac{n+2}{p}}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j}).\end{array}

Thus, using
Eq.

8.15
, we get

E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j+1})\leq C(n,p)\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\lambda^{\max\{0,2-p\}}\mathbf{F}_{j}+2^{3+\frac{n+2}{p}}\gamma E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j}).

(8.21)

We take
\displaystyle\gamma=\frac{1}{2^{4+\frac{n+2}{p}}}
which in turn fixes
\displaystyle\sigma=\sigma(n,p)
according to
Eq.

8.20
so that
Eq.

8.21
becomes

E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j+1})\leq\frac{1}{2}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j})+\lambda^{\max\{0,2-p\}}\mathbf{F}_{j}.

(8.22)

It follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\sum_{j=j_{0}}^{j_{1}}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j+1})&\leq&E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j_{0}})+C\sum_{j=j_{0}}^{j_{1}}\lambda^{\max\{0,2-p\}}\mathbf{F}_{j}\\
&\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{km},\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{ind_diff}}{\leq}&\frac{1}{64}\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{p}}\lambda+\mathbf{C}\frac{\lambda}{\mathbf{H}_{2}^{\min\{p-1,1\}}}\\
&\leq&\frac{1}{32}\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{\frac{n+2}{p}}\lambda,\end{array}

(8.23)

provided
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}=\mathbf{H}_{2}(n,p)
is large enough. Since there holds

|(\nabla u)_{Q_{j+1}}-(\nabla u)_{Q_{j}}|\leq\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+1}}|\nabla u-(\nabla u)_{Q_{j}}|\ dz\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j+1}}|\nabla u-(\nabla u)_{Q_{j}}|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j}),

we have

|(\nabla u)_{Q_{j_{1}+1}}|\leq|(\nabla u)_{Q_{j_{0}}}|+\left(\frac{\sigma}{2}\right)^{-\frac{n+2}{p}}\sum_{j=j_{0}}^{j_{1}}E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j})\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{decay}}{\leq}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j_{0}}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\frac{1}{32}\lambda\overset{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{km}}{\leq}\frac{1}{16}\lambda.

(8.24)

Hence, combining
Eq.

8.23
and
Eq.

8.24
along with the fact that
\displaystyle\sigma\in(0,\tfrac{1}{4})
, we obtain

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{j_{1}+1}}|\nabla u|^{p}\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j_{1}+1})\leq|(\nabla u)_{Q_{j_{1}+1}}|+2E_{p}(\nabla u,Q_{j_{1}+1})\leq\lambda.

This completes the proof of claim.
∎

8.6
Proof of
Theorem

2.7

The proof of
Proposition

8.13
requires the hypothesis
Eq.

8.3
hold, which we will prove in this subsection, thus completing the proof of the theorem. Let
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{1}\geq 1
and
\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{2}\geq 1
be fixed as in
Proposition

8.13
and we show the existence of
\displaystyle\lambda\geq 1
satisfying
Eq.

8.3
as follows:

Case
\displaystyle p\geq 2
:

For
\displaystyle\mu\geq 1
, let us define

h(\mu):=\mu^{p}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\mu}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{p}\right).

Then we see that
\displaystyle h(\mu)
is a continuous function satisfying
\displaystyle h(1)<0
and
\displaystyle\lim_{\mu\to\infty}h(\mu)=\infty
because there holds

\begin{array}[]{rcl}h(\mu)&=&\mu^{p}-\left(\mu^{p-2}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\frac{1}{|Q_{2\rho}|}\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\mu}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{p}\right)\\
&\geq&\mu^{p}-\left(\mu^{p-2}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{p}\right).\end{array}

Thus there exists
\displaystyle\lambda>1
such that
\displaystyle h(\lambda)=0
or in particular, the hypothesis
Eq.

8.3
holds.
Also,
\displaystyle h(\lambda)=0
implies
\displaystyle\lambda^{p}\geq\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{p}
and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lambda^{p}=\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{p}\leq\lambda^{p-2}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\lambda^{p-1}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F},\end{array}

which gives

\lambda^{2}\leq\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\lambda\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F}\leq\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{H}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{F}^{2}.

Thus, we obtain

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lambda\apprle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbf{F}.\end{array}

(8.25)

Substituting
Eq.

8.25
into
Proposition

8.13
gives

|\nabla u(z_{0})|\leq\lambda\apprle_{(n,p)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbf{F}.

Case
\displaystyle\frac{2n}{n+2}<p<2
:

Similarly, for
\displaystyle\mu\geq 1
and
\displaystyle\rho^{\mu}:=\mu^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\rho
, we define

h(\mu):=\mu^{p}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho^{\mu}}^{\mu}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\mathbf{H}_{2}^{p}\mathbf{F}^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\right).

Analogous to the degenerate case, we can find
\displaystyle\lambda>\max\{1,\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F}^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\}
such that
\displaystyle h(\lambda)=0
.
Since there holds that

0<\frac{p}{d}=\frac{p(n+2)-2n}{2}=p-\frac{(2-p)n}{2},

we see that

\lambda^{p}\leq\lambda^{p-\frac{p}{d}}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\lambda^{p-\frac{p}{d}}\left(\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F}^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\right)^{\frac{p}{d}}.

Thus, we get

\lambda^{\frac{p}{d}}\leq\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\left(\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F}^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\right)^{\frac{p}{d}}.

Combining with
Proposition

8.13
, we obtain

|\nabla u(z_{0})|\apprle_{(n,p)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.55559pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-13.52937pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-11.41374pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-10.66374pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz\right)^{\frac{d}{p}}+\mathbf{F}^{\frac{1}{p-1}}.

Remark 8.15
.

We remark that
Eq.

8.3
is equivalent to

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{p}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\textstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-15.86662pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-14.04051pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.90251pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle\scriptscriptstyle\rotatebox[origin]{18.0}{$\displaystyle-\mkern-9.5mu-$}$}}\kern-9.15251pt}}\!\iint_{Q_{2\rho}^{\lambda}}|\nabla u|^{p}+1\ dz+\lambda^{\max\{1,p-1\}}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{F}=\lambda^{p}.

Applying Young’s inequality, one can recover
Lemma

8.7
and
Corollary

8.11
. The Proof of
Theorem

2.7
also follows analogously.
